# Skills · S · part 26

754 entries, sci-writing to scientific-brainstorming. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sci-writing**: Draft manuscript sections, format citations, and get AI peer review. Natural language invocation -- describe what you need and the skill routes to drafting, citation formatting, or peer review. Reads research profile, data analysis outputs, and literature search results for context-aware writing. After drafting, offers to generate supporting figures via viz skills. Triggers on: "draft introduction", "write methods", "format citations", "APA style", "bibliography", "peer review", "review my draft", "write abstract". Does NOT trigger for: literature search (use sci-literature-research), data an… ([krmdel/organon](https://github.com/krmdel/organon/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sci-writing) · ★8)
- **sci-writing**: 撰写、改写或润色英文期刊论文及投稿材料；适用于各论文章节、学术表达、伦理声明、投稿信与审稿回复。 ([Zenine/SCI-Writingskill](https://github.com/Zenine/SCI-Writingskill/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-writing) · ★2)
- **sci-writing-and-revision**: 已有科研结果，需要构建论文主线与 Figure 证据顺序、撰写或修改 SCI 原稿、处理审稿意见、回复信和 cover letter 时使用。适用于普通 SCI 或一般生命科学稿件；不用于 Nature/CNS 专门写作、正式期刊回复或完整论文流水线。Figure 数量、叙事结构和补实验强度由核心 claim、证据和目标期刊真实要求决定；不固定六图、A-B-C-D、rescue 或期刊分数。 ([xiaojinlucky/skills](https://github.com/xiaojinlucky/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-writing-and-revision) · ★1)
- **sci-zotero**: Interact with your Zotero library to sync references, add citations by DOI/ISBN/PMID, and manage PDFs. Triggers on Zotero-related requests. ([ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write](https://github.com/ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-zotero) · ★225)
- **sci2doc**: 用于将已成稿的 SCI 论文材料转化为中文博士或硕士学位论文草稿。技能采用原子化 Markdown 工作流，逐节撰写后合并导出 Word，执行严格的章节结构校验、逐阶段门禁检查与版本快照回滚。正文字数按学位类型设定软目标（博士不低于五万字，硕士不低于三万字），材料不足时宁可少写，不以编造数据充抵字数。格式支持内置模板与自定义院校模板两种模式，自定义模板信息不完整时仅允许整理 Markdown，不予导出 docx。触发词：博士论文、硕士论文、学位论文、毕业论文、SCI转论文、doctoral thesis、master thesis、dissertation。本技能要求用户提供可访问的 SCI 论文材料，不适用于本科毕业论文。 ([wsxwj123/opencode-skills-backup](https://github.com/wsxwj123/opencode-skills-backup/tree/HEAD/sci2doc) · ★7)

- **sci_academic_translation_polishing**: 针对物理、工程及材料科学领域的学术文本进行翻译或润色。翻译时严格保持原文结构与含义；润色时优化句式与流畅度，并可兼顾实际应用背景。均需符合SCI期刊发表标准。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/ConvSkill/chinese_gpt4_8/sci_academic_translation_polishing) · ★620 · 4 copies)

- **scib-integration-benchmark-scoring**: Use when two or more integration methods (e.g. SpatialMETA, Seurat RPCA/CCA, scVI, scANVI, totalVI, Stabmap, scPoli, spaVAE, PCA) have produced latent embeddings of the same joint ST-SM AnnData and a fair, metric-based ranking of batch correction vs. biological conservation is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/scib-integration-benchmark-scoring))
- **scib-metric-aggregation-and-ranking**: Use when you have joint embeddings from multiple integration methods (e.g. SpatialMETA, totalVI, MISO, SpatialGLUE) on the same ST-SM samples and need to quantitatively rank their integration quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/scib-metric-aggregation-and-ranking))
- **scib-metric-computation**: Use when multiple data-integration or label-transfer methods have been run on the same reference-query split and you need a ranked, metric-based comparison of their embeddings (e.g., on a pan-cancer tumor-infiltrating myeloid AnnData). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scib-metric-computation))
- **scibench-eval**: This benchmark evaluates large language models' ability to solve college-level scientific problems across mathematics, chemistry, and physics. It probes multi-step quantitative reasoning, unit conversion, physical derivations, and the efficacy of prompting strategies and external computational tools. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SciBench, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/scibench-eval) · ★3)
- **scibet-multinomial-classification**: Use when a labeled scRNA-seq reference exists and cell types must be predicted for newly sequenced cells or clusters, including cross-platform transfer (full-length vs UMI, e.g., 6 ordered human pancreas training-test pairs), with benchmarking against Seurat v3 and scmapCluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scibet-multinomial-classification))
- **scibox-diagram**: 制作与修改可编辑的 draw.io / diagrams.net 示意图（.drawio XML），产出 .drawio + PNG/PDF。三条路径：套用内置模板（五带技术路线图、三栏研究框架图、三栏阶段流程图、横版任务流水线图）、从零手写 XML、高保真复刻参考图。当用户要求技术路线图、全文概览、研究框架图、论文流程图、算法流程图、模型/系统架构图、方法示意图、把论文或课题做成一张图、答辩用图，或要求画 drawio 图、照着某张图重画成可编辑矢量图、修图（文字溢出/箭头错乱/配色不一致/排版对不齐）时使用。画折线图热图等数据图表请改用 scibox-figure。 ([yuanchen-home/cumcm-step-review](https://github.com/yuanchen-home/cumcm-step-review/tree/HEAD/tools/drawio/scibox-diagram) · ★317 · 2 copies)

- **scienceworld-object-classifier**: Moves a tested or examined object into a designated container (e.g., a specific colored box) based on a determined property. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/scientific-agent-skills-scientific-writing) · ★1)

Use for directional trajectory hypotheses and kinetic-model diagnostics alongside Scanpy; velocity alone does not establish cell fate or causal drivers.
